\section{What is tabulated}

A degenerate vibrational mode $\Gamma_s$ of dimension $d$ has creation operators $a^\dagger_1,\ldots,a^\dagger_d$, and its $v$-quantum states span $\mathrm{Sym}^v\Gamma_s$. The point group acts by Lemma~2 of the paper, $U(R)\,a^\dagger_iU(R)^\dagger=\sum_jD_{ji}(R)\,a^\dagger_j$, and the symmetry-adapted states (SALCs) $\ket{{}^\mu\Gamma;k}$ are obtained with the matrix-valued projectors $P^\Gamma_{\mu\nu}=(d_\Gamma/|\mathsf G|)\sum_RD^\Gamma_{\mu\nu}(R)^*U(R)$ (Theorem~1): copies $k$ by projecting occupation states in order, partners by $P^\Gamma_{\mu1}$. This is the construction of \texttt{salc\_fock\_basis.wl}. The groups, irrep matrices, and Mulliken labels are those of \texttt{species\_tables/pointgroups.py}, so the vibrational carriers here are in the same bases as the rotational and nuclear-spin carriers of the species tables.

\emph{Kets.} Two-dimensional modes are written $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ with vibrational angular momentum $\ell=n_+-n_-$; a ${}^1E$ quantum is counted in $n_-$ ($\ell=-1$) and a ${}^2E$ quantum in $n_+$, and $U(C_n)=e^{-2\pi i\ell/n}$. Three-dimensional modes are written $\ket{n_xn_yn_z}$ in the Cartesian basis. Coefficients are exact where they are recognised ($\omega=e^{2\pi i/3}$); the remaining icosahedral ones are given to four decimals.

\section{Three classifications}

A table for every $v$ is unnecessary, because the SALCs of all $v$ follow from a finite amount of data. Three facts reduce it.

\begin{proposition}[Parity]
The vibrational angular momentum ($\ell$ for $d=2$, $L$ for vector-like $d=3$) has the parity of $v$. An inversion or $\sigma_h$ acts on $v$ quanta of a $u$ or $''$ mode as $(-1)^v$; hence $g/u$ and $'/''$ labels alternate with $v$ for such modes and are constant for $g$ and $'$ modes.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Residues, $d=2$]
Every group element acts on a two-dimensional mode as $s\,\mathrm{diag}(e^{i\theta},e^{-i\theta})$ or $s$ times a swap, with $s=\pm1$. Hence it maps $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ to a multiple of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ or $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, every SALC lies in $\mathrm{span}\{\ket{n_+,n_-},\ket{n_-,n_+}\}$, and its species and coefficients depend only on $\ell$ modulo a period $N'$, the least common multiple of the orders of the nonzero matrix entries of the mode: the phase of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ under an element is such an entry to the power $-\ell$, because $v-\ell$ is even. One table with $N'$ rows therefore gives the SALCs for every $v$.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Harmonics, vector-like $d=3$]
If $D(R)=\varepsilon(R)\,R$ for a one-dimensional character $\varepsilon$, the invariant $a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger=\sum_ia^{\dagger2}_i$ and its adjoint commute with $U(R)$, and
\[
\mathrm{Sym}^v=\bigoplus_{L=v,v-2,\ldots}(a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger)^{(v-L)/2}\,\mathcal H_L,\qquad \mathcal H_L=\ker(a\cdot a)\cap\mathrm{Sym}^L,\quad\dim\mathcal H_L=2L+1 .
\]
Every SALC of $v$ quanta is $\mathcal N_{v,L}(a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger)^{k}\psi_L$, $k=(v-L)/2$, with $\psi_L$ a $\mathsf G$-adapted harmonic SALC and $\mathcal N_{v,L}^{-2}=4^kk!\,(L+\tfrac32)_k$. The species of $\mathcal H_L$ are the rotational branching $D^{L}|_{\mathsf G}$ with $\mathsf O(3)$ parity $(-1)^L$, times $\varepsilon^L$: the same multiplicities as the rotational sector of the paper, and the copy label of a repeated irrep becomes the layer $L$.
\end{proposition}

The first two are elementary; the third is the isotropic-oscillator decomposition restricted to $\mathsf G$. Modes covered by neither (the $T_2$, $G$, $H$ modes of $I$ and $I_h$) are listed explicitly for small $v$, together with their multiplicities to $v=12$.

\emph{Checks.} \texttt{salc\_tables.py} stops unless all of the following hold. Each residue table is compared with the projector construction for every $v\le2N'+2$ and every $\ell$, and the resulting states are verified to be orthonormal, complete, and covariant. Each harmonic table is verified the same way for $L\le4$, the norm formula and the factorised SALCs are verified for $v\le4$, and the harmonic species are computed from $\mathrm{Sym}^L-\mathrm{Sym}^{L-2}$. Every explicit table is verified for orthonormality, completeness, and covariance. The multiplicities agree with GTPack for 32 crystallographic groups. In all, the tables cover 80 two-dimensional modes, 67 separably degenerate pairs, 14 vector-like three-dimensional modes, and 9 further modes.


\section{Worked examples at finite $v$}\label{sec:examples}

Explicit SALCs of one mode for small $v$, from the projector construction; each set is orthonormal, complete, and transforms with the irrep matrices. They are special cases of the tables of Sec.~\ref{sec:groups}.

\clearpage\section{All point groups}\label{sec:groups}

For every group of the species tables: residue tables for two-dimensional modes (for $\ell>0$ each row gives the SALC on the pair $\ket{n_+,n_-}$, $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, partners separated by commas), single-row residue lists for separably degenerate pairs, harmonic tables for vector-like three-dimensional modes, and explicit tables otherwise. Linear molecules: for $C_{\infty v}$ and $D_{\infty h}$ the residue rule holds with $N'\to\infty$, the pair $\ell=\pm\Lambda$ forming the $\Lambda$ ($\Pi,\Delta,\Phi,\ldots$) species with partners $\ket{n_+,n_-}$, $\ket{n_-,n_+}$, and $\ell=0$ giving $\Sigma^+$; for $D_{\infty h}$ the parity is $(-1)^v$ for a $\Pi_u$ mode.

\subsection*{$C_1$ \quad (order 1)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$C_1$}

All irreps are one-dimensional and real: $\ket{n}$ of a mode $\Gamma_s$ has species $\Gamma_s^{\otimes n}$.

\hline\end{longtable}

\subsection*{$T$ \quad (order 12)}

\addcontentsline{toc}{subsection}{$T$}

\emph{Separably degenerate mode ${}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E$.} Every occupation state $\ket{n_+,n_-}$ is a SALC; its species depends only on $\ell\bmod 3$ (all integers $\ell$):
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $\ell\bmod 3$ & species of $\ket{n_+,n_-}$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $A$\\
1 & ${}^{2}E$\\
2 & ${}^{1}E$\\
\hline\end{longtable}

\emph{Mode $T$, vector-like: $D(R)=\varepsilon(R)\,R$ with $\varepsilon=A$.} Every SALC is $\mathcal N_{v,L}\,(a^\dagger\!\cdot a^\dagger)^{(v-L)/2}\psi_L$ with $\psi_L$ a harmonic SALC below and $\mathcal N_{v,L}^{-2}=4^kk!\,(L+\tfrac32)_k$, $k=(v-L)/2$. Species of the harmonic layer $\mathcal H_L$:
\begin{longtable}{c l}\hline $L$ & $\mathcal H_L$\\ \hline\endhead
0 & $A$\\
1 & $T$\\
2 & ${}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus T$\\
3 & $A\oplus 2\,T$\\
4 & $A\oplus {}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus 2\,T$\\
5 & ${}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus 3\,T$\\
6 & $2\,A\oplus {}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus 3\,T$\\
7 & $A\oplus {}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus 4\,T$\\
8 & $A\oplus 2\,{}^{1}E\oplus 2\,{}^{2}E\oplus 4\,T$\\
9 & $2\,A\oplus {}^{1}E\oplus {}^{2}E\oplus 5\,T$\\
10 & $2\,A\oplus 2\,{}^{1}E\oplus 2\,{}^{2}E\oplus 5\,T$\\
11 & $A\oplus 2\,{}^{1}E\oplus 2\,{}^{2}E\oplus 6\,T$\\
12 & $3\,A\oplus 2\,{}^{1}E\oplus 2\,{}^{2}E\oplus 6\,T$\\
\hline\end{longtable}
Harmonic SALCs $\psi_L$, $L\le4$, in $\ket{n_xn_yn_z}$:
